
`✓` indicates correctly rounded for all four rounding modes; ULP values list the largest recorded error; `N/A` marks variants not defined for that format.  `long double` (`*l`) entrypoints alias `double`, `f80`, `double-double`, or `_Float128` according to the target ABI, and the `bfloat16` `log` entrypoint is named `log_bf16` to avoid colliding with `_Float16` `logb` (`logbf16`).  See {doc}`../math/index` for accuracy goals, GPU conformance, performance benchmarks, and algorithm notes.
